## Foundational objective — compatible reduction density
The motivating paper is Inoue, Kawarabayashi, Miyashita, Mohar, Thomassen and Thorup, The Four Color Theorem with Linearly Many Reducible Configurations and Near-Linear Time Coloring, arXiv:2603.24880v3 (1 October 2026), https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24880v3. Its abstract establishes linearly many pairwise non-touching D-reducible configurations or pairwise non-crossing obstructing cycles of length at most five in planar triangulations, including flat regions, leading to constant-factor reduction and O(n log n) coloring. It is not a general graph reduction theorem or P=NP.
H15 is a core research objective, not an optional appendix. Keep original implementation order but run an independent H15 source-audit/benchmark lane in parallel once the local codec cube is available. Distinguish observer-only contraction from lossless encoding and solver witness extension. H15a audit actual paper configurations and reduction/reconstruction algorithm (OPEN); H15b define compatible supports and local reconstruction certificates (OPEN); H15c prove density on explicit native carrier classes, with flat-region and obstruction controls (OPEN); H15d measure contraction including interfaces, residuals and costs (OPEN); H15e establish geometric recurrence and end-to-end clock (OPEN); H15f test original MAX-CUT boundary score/threshold and witness transport without assuming planarity or bounded interface (OPEN). Six-face commutation does not establish density, and cardinality reduction does not establish speedup. Paper full configuration catalogue remains unaudited.

## Contracts
Use separate history h, physical time t, refinement k, Frobenius exponent j, consumer version v. Retain chart marking, frame and residual in dependent fibres; do not erase history. Balanced ternary T={-1,0,+1}; 111 is an address, positional value 13 only under the specified reading. T^n is equivalent to the dependent sum over binary support masks of sign payloads indexed by active count. Prefix truncation alone is lossy; prefix plus suffix roundtrips.

A cube has eight typed vertices, twelve edges, six square faces; prove six monotone endpoint paths agree by adjacent swaps when all faces commute. Residual faces need typed transport and reconstruction, not fabricated equality. Time/frame defect U_(t+1) P_t(x)-P_t(U_t x); for linear U, transform residual by U_(t+1) and predictor by conjugation with time-specific frames. Distinguish residual of original evolution from commutation defect.

Route A: marked AGr(2,4) x SO(3), local dimension 6+3 only if independent frame, not gauge redundance; finite proper cube rotations and C3 axis action, then Bishop continuous lift.
Route B: AGr(2,5), six slopes + three offsets in graph chart over F3. Ten marked coordinate charts, 196830 parameterizations, 32670 distinct affine planes (Gaussian binomial count). Retain chart marking for reconstruction; 83.4% fewer geometric evaluations only for chart-invariant consumers, not a lossless bitrate claim.
Route C: notation proposal with vertically stacked p,n,j (characteristic, extension degree, Frobenius iteration); distinguish vector space F_p^n from field F_(p^n). Frob(p,n,j)(x)=x^(p^j). Candidate GF(3^9) modulus t^9+t^4-1, candidate normal generator t^5; independently check irreducibility, rank, direction convention and full conjugacy. Relative Frob^3 has 27 fixed points; GF(8) x GF(3^9) diagonal C3 has 54 fixed points and 52470 free orbits. GF(3^6) is not a subfield of GF(3^9); shared GF(27). No Monster action from counts.
CMYKA: alpha retained. For additive premultiplied colour, retain moments A, AC, AK, ACK and analogues M,Y plus sample weight. Ordered segment composition (c,tau)*(d,sigma)=(c+tau*d,tau*sigma) associative, not commutative. No naive CMYK aggregation or reordering.
Tetration: I_0=unit, I_(n+1)=I_n -> Fin 9, N_(n+1)=9^N_n; separate from product width 3/6/9, N_(k+1)=27N_k. Sparse program evaluator with typed residual and execution clock; no dense enumeration or generic compression guarantee.
